A school fitness program kids actually love

Confidence, focus and fitness — built on boxing skills. Active, structured and genuinely fun. No contact, no sparring.

Enquire for Your School
The Program

What to expect from our sessions

💪

Strong Body

Footwork, coordination, fitness and conditioning. Physical literacy that builds confidence in movement — skills that carry across every sport and every stage of life.

🧠

Strong Mind

Discipline, mental toughness and focus built into every drill. Kids arrive energised and carry that sharpness straight into the classroom.

🎓

Strong Future

Resilience, character and the belief that hard work pays off. The skills learned here go well beyond the session — they're built for life.

How It Works

Three steps to Fight Forward

1

You Enquire

Fill in the form or give us a call. Quick conversation to see if Fight Forward fits your enrichment program.

2

Free Trial Session

We come to your school and run a completely free trial. No commitment, no cost.

3

Term Program Begins

8-week terms, before school, 45 minutes. Parents enrol and pay directly — no cost to your school.
